- **weather**: Fetch 기상청 동네예보 (KMA village weather forecast) from data.go.kr (service 1360000) -- the 단기예보 (`forecast`, ~3 days), 초단기예보 (`ultra_forecast`, 6 hours), and 초단기실황 (`nowcast`, latest observation) for one 5km grid cell (nx, ny). Holds no logic of its own -- it calls the pydatagokr package's CLI (`datagokr weather`) and shows the result to the user. Trigger phrases: 날씨, 동네예보, 단기예보, 초단기예보, 기온, 강수확률, 하늘상태, weather forecast, temperature, rain probability. Use when connecting to Weaviate Cloud; not for local Docker instances without auth. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/weaviate-mcp-401-unauthorized-on-weavi-10c73465f924d50c))
- **weaviate-mcp-collection-not-found-fix-6397c250c19f28d4**: Fixes the Weaviate MCP server failing queries with collection not found. The collection (class) name in the query does not exist on the instance. The fix is listing collections and correcting the name, respecting Weaviate's capitalization rules. Use when the server connects but a specific collection is rejected; not for connection errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/weaviate-mcp-collection-not-found-fix-6397c250c19f28d4))
- **weaviate-mcp-queries-hang-grpc-port-50-59bf0d6b2ee8e480**: Fixes the Weaviate MCP server hanging or failing on queries while simple REST calls work. Weaviate uses gRPC on port 50051 for vector operations, and Docker setups often do not publish it. The fix is mapping port 50051 in the Docker run. Use when REST works but queries stall; not for 401s. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/weaviate-mcp-queries-hang-grpc-port-50-59bf0d6b2ee8e480))
